podgreatpersons.0001 = {
	type = character_event
	title = podgreatpersons.0001.t
	desc = podgreatpersons.0001.desc
	theme = greatperson 
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:gp_ald
		animation = personality_rational
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Abd al-Latif al-Baghdadi" 
			age = 69 
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			learning = 10
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:bedouin
			trait = education_learning_4
			trait = intellect_good_1
			trait = diligent
			trait = fickle
			trait = gregarious
			trait = scholar
			trait = theologian
			trait = physician_3
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_ald
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_the_physician
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_ald
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_ald 
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ald
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ald
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_ald = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_ald		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}	
		scope:gp_ald = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}	
}

						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}

						flag = gp_pompadour
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_pompadour
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}

	{

		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pompadour
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_pompadour = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}

						years = 2

		}

			dynasty = none
			stewardship = 10
			diplomacy = 5
			learning = 5
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:italian
			trait = education_stewardship_4
			trait = gregarious
			trait = diligent
			trait = patient
			trait = adventurer
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_polo
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_master_explorer
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_polo = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_polo
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_polo
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_polo
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_polo = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_polo
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_polo = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_polo = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_polo		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}

			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {

		}

	desc = podgreatpersons.0052.desc
	theme = greatperson 
	left_portrait = {
		character = scope:gp_pons
		animation = flirtation
		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Pons de Capduelh" 
			age = 46
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			diplomacy = 10
			learning = 5
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:occitan
			trait = education_diplomacy_3
			trait = content
			trait = stubborn
			trait = gregarious
			trait = gallant
			trait = mortal
			random_traits = no		
			location = root.capital_province	
			save_scope_as = gp_pons
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_popular_troubadour
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_pons
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_pons
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a	
		
		remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pons
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}	
			}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}			
	}	
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.b
		
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
	
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom	
	
		hidden_effect = {
	
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pons
		POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
						years = 2
					}
				}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}	

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.c
		
		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.customb
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		scope:gp_pons = {
			move_to_pool = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}		
	}

	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.d
		
		trigger = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_pons		
		
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}	
		scope:gp_pons = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
			}
		scope:vampiresire = {
			POD_player_character_create_vampirechilde_effect = yes			
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}	
}

		}
	
	trigger = { 
		}
	
	immediate =	
	{		
		create_character = {
			name = "Tomas de Torquemada" 
			age = 71
			gender = male
			dynasty = none
			intrigue = 10
			faith = faith:innocence
			culture = culture:castilian
			trait = education_intrigue_3
			trait = zealous
			trait = arbitrary
			trait = sadistic
			trait = torturer

			random_traits = no

			save_scope_as = gp_torquemada
			after_creation = {
			give_nickname = nick_hammer_heretics
			}
		}
		
		scope:gp_torquemada = {
				#POD_clear_resonance_effect = yes
				POD_set_resonance_effect = yes
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = POD_great_person_flag
				}
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = gp_torquemada
					}
				}			
				
		set_global_variable = {
			name = spawned_gp_torquemada
			value = yes
		}				
	
		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_positive_effect"	
	}
	
	option = 
	{
		name = podgreatpersons.0001.a

		custom_tooltip = podgreatpersons.0001.custom
		
		hidden_effect = {
		
		add_courtier = scope:gp_torquemada

		scope:gp_torquemada = {
				add_character_flag = {
						flag = blocked_from_leaving
